Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in general theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 46 and the invariant I(A,B) = 544 mod 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_46(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 46. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^544(S, F) is trivial. QED.
